### M11

M11: the existing outputs cannot distinguish a flattened from an unflattened sine at the threshold (characterization design section 10.4).

### Fundamental source

Fundamental source: characterization uses the generated fundamental; the product uses the user-declared fundamental and does not check it (section 10.6).
